# AGENT_PROMPT — Open Bot Avatar

## Piece

- id: `open-bot-avatar`
- title: Open Bot Avatar
- subtitle: One live avatar. State, mood, finish, preset, and colour.

Dominik Scholz (@dom_scholz). Post: https://x.com/dom_scholz/status/2117307304395636772. Repo: https://github.com/DominikScholz/open-bot-avatar. Site: https://dominikscholz.github.io/open-bot-avatar/. The library is MIT, Copyright (c) 2026 Dominik Scholz. The copied document carries that full licence.

## Stage content

A 960×720 stage, background #f3f3f4. The left side holds one square avatar, 420px, the gallery Cat in #f8a01c, fluffy, idle, neutral, following the pointer. The right side is a 400px panel: six states, mood, material, a preset list, a colour row, and a JSON box with Import. The credit links Dominik Scholz and @dom_scholz, plus the post, the repo, and the site. The visible words are Dominik Scholz, @dom_scholz, post, repo, and site.

## Geometry and values

- Stage: width 960px, height 720px, background #f3f3f4. The type is system-ui.
- Avatar host: class `oba-avatar-host`, width 420px, max-width calc(100% - 48px), aspect-ratio 1. It fills with one canvas.
- Panel: width 400px, padding 16px, border-left 1px solid rgba(23, 24, 28, 0.08).
- States, in a 3-column grid: Idle, Thinking, Working, Waiting, Blocked, Done. Idle starts checked.
- Mood: Neutral, Happy. Neutral starts checked.
- Material: Flat, Fluffy. Fluffy starts checked.
- Presets: Examples (Controller, Barbell, Mug, Lightbulb, Chat, Sprout, Watering Can) and Gallery (Book, Brain, Bull, Bunny, Camera, Cat, Cloud, Egg, Ghost, Magnifier, Paint Brush, Palette, Rocket, Shield, Star, Witch Hat). Cat starts checked. The preset list scrolls inside a 216px box.
- Colour row, in order: White #f4f4f4, Brown #a8693f, Red #f2384b, Orange #ff6a1a, Amber #f8a01c, Green #1fbf68, Teal #14b3a5, Blue #1f7cf2, Purple #8b5cf6, Pink #ec4899, Gray #7a7a7a. Amber starts checked. Each swatch is 22×22.
- JSON: a textarea, min-height 64px, and an Import button, height 30px. Empty until someone pastes.
- Credit: 12px, line-height 16px, colour rgba(23, 24, 28, 0.62), centred 12px from the bottom of the avatar column.

## States

| State | Trigger | From | To | Duration | Easing | Delay |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| idle | Idle | the current pose | the idle pose | 420ms | smoothstep | |
| thinking | Thinking | the current pose | three hopping dots | 420ms | smoothstep | |
| working | Working | the current pose | the working pose | 420ms | smoothstep | |
| waiting | Waiting | the current pose | the waiting pose | 420ms | smoothstep | |
| blocked | Blocked | the current pose | an exclamation mark | 420ms | smoothstep | |
| done | Done | the current pose | the done pose | 420ms | smoothstep | |
| mood | Neutral or Happy | the current eyes | the other mood | 420ms | smoothstep | |
| material | Flat or Fluffy | the current finish | the other finish | the next frame | | |
| preset | a preset chip | the current avatar | that preset, in its own colour | the next frame | | |
| colour | a colour swatch | the current colour | that swatch | the next frame | | |
| import | Import, with valid JSON | the current avatar | the validated definition | the next frame | | |
| import error | Import, with invalid JSON or invalid syntax | the current avatar | the same avatar, with the errors listed | | | |
| pointer | the pointer moves over the page | the current gaze | the pointer | follows the pointer | | |
| reduced | prefers-reduced-motion: reduce, or data-reduce-motion="1" | a moving pose | the pose at once | 0ms | | |

## Timing

A change of state or mood eases for 420ms with smoothstep. Reduced motion snaps that change. The pointer updates the gaze on the animation frame. `done` turns for 3.87s inside a 6.2s cycle when motion is on. Under reduced motion the ribbons are left out, the marks hold still, and `done` does not turn.

## Reduced motion

`prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` and `data-reduce-motion="1"` on the document both count. Either one snaps state and mood changes, leaves the ribbons out, holds the marks still, and keeps `done` from turning. The eyes can still look at the pointer. The stage does not add a second clock.

## Gotchas

- The copied document is one HTML file. Its script is the renderer and this stage, bundled. The credit anchors are external URLs, and nothing fetches them. The document is 960px wide and 720px tall.
- The MIT text, Copyright (c) 2026 Dominik Scholz, is in that file, including "Permission is hereby granted, free of charge" and "THE SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED \"AS IS\"". Stripping it is a failed copy.
- The type is system-ui. The document does not request a font.
- Fluffy paints fur on a WebGL2 canvas. Without WebGL2 the same avatar draws flat.
- Import runs `validateAvatar` on the parsed JSON. Invalid JSON syntax is the parser message. A definition that fails validation lists each error and leaves the avatar as it was.
- A known preset id imported from JSON selects that preset and its colour. Any other valid id shows as Imported and keeps the current colour.
- The avatar follows the pointer. It does not turn when the stage is dragged.

## Setup

Mount the HTML file. It has no install step and no run command. The script listens for prefers-reduced-motion and for data-reduce-motion="1".

## Known gaps

The card does not reflow for phone widths. Desktop 1280 is the gate.

It is a playground for one avatar, with no Generate, file-drop import, export menus or share links.

There is no listening or speaking state, so the six states are the full set.

Fluffy needs WebGL2 and otherwise draws flat.

The preview does not rotate on drag.

Checked in desktop Chrome only. Safari was not checked.

## Checklist

- [ ] Cat starts, in #f8a01c, fluffy, idle, and neutral. The pointer moves the gaze.
- [ ] Idle, Thinking, Working, Waiting, Blocked, and Done each show. Neutral and Happy each show. Flat and Fluffy each show.
- [ ] Controller, Mug, and Sprout each replace the avatar and take their own colour. Book, Ghost, and Rocket do the same.
- [ ] Amber, Blue, and White each recolour the body.
- [ ] This definition imports and draws: `{"version":1,"id":"box","name":"Box","body":{"path":"M20 20 H80 V80 H20 Z"},"eyes":{"x":50,"y":46,"spacing":15,"scale":1}}`. `{ "version": 1 }` lists the errors and leaves the avatar in place. `{` lists a syntax error.
- [ ] Reduced motion snaps the pose. The credit shows Dominik Scholz and @dom_scholz as links, and also links the post, the repo, and the site. No address is written out in the stage.
- [ ] The document includes the MIT text and does not request a font, a script, or an image.

## Code

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8" />
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1" />
<title>Open Bot Avatar</title>

<style>
.oba {
  width: 960px;
  height: 720px;
  display: flex;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  background: #f3f3f4;
  color: #17181c;
  font-family: system-ui, -apple-system, "Segoe UI", Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif;
}
.oba-stage {
  position: relative;
  flex: 1 1 auto;
  min-width: 0;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
}
.oba-avatar-host {
  width: 420px;
  max-width: calc(100% - 48px);
  aspect-ratio: 1;
}
.oba-compact {
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  min-width: 0;
}
.oba-compact .oba-stage { width: 100%; height: 100%; }
.oba-compact .oba-avatar-host {
  width: 100%;
  max-width: none;
  height: 100%;
}
.oba-panel {
  width: 400px;
  flex: none;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  height: 100%;
  padding: 16px;
  overflow: auto;
  border-left: 1px solid rgba(23, 24, 28, 0.08);
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 12px;
}
.oba-name {
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 13px;
  line-height: 18px;
  color: rgba(23, 24, 28, 0.72);
}
.oba-field {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 6px;
  margin: 0;
}
.oba-label {
  font-size: 11px;
  line-height: 14px;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.06em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: rgba(23, 24, 28, 0.52);
}
.oba-states {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
  gap: 6px;
}
.oba-row {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 6px;
}
.oba-chip {
  appearance: none;
  flex: none;
  height: 30px;
  padding: 0 10px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  border: 1px solid rgba(23, 24, 28, 0.12);
  background: #ffffff;
  color: #17181c;
  font: 500 13px/1 system-ui, -apple-system, "Segoe UI", Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif;
  cursor: pointer;
}
.oba-states .oba-chip { width: 100%; }
.oba-chip[aria-checked="true"] {
  background: #17181c;
  border-color: #17181c;
  color: #ffffff;
}
.oba-chip:hover { background: #f1f1f1; }
.oba-chip[aria-checked="true"]:hover { background: #3a3a3e; }
.oba-presets {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 8px;
  max-height: 216px;
  overflow: auto;
}
.oba-preset-group {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 6px;
}
.oba-group-name {
  font-size: 12px;
  line-height: 16px;
  color: rgba(23, 24, 28, 0.62);
}
.oba-swatches {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 8px;
}
.oba-swatch {
  width: 22px;
  height: 22px;
  padding: 0;
  border-radius: 999px;
  border: 1px solid rgba(23, 24, 28, 0.18);
  cursor: pointer;
}
.oba-swatch[aria-checked="true"] {
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px #f3f3f4, 0 0 0 4px #17181c;
}
.oba-json {
  width: 100%;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  flex: none;
  min-height: 64px;
  height: 64px;
  resize: vertical;
  border-radius: 10px;
  border: 1px solid rgba(23, 24, 28, 0.12);
  background: #ffffff;
  color: #17181c;
  padding: 8px 10px;
  font: 400 12px/16px ui-monospace, "SFMono-Regular", Menlo, Consolas, monospace;
}
.oba-import {
  appearance: none;
  align-self: flex-start;
  flex: none;
  height: 30px;
  padding: 0 12px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  border: 1px solid #17181c;
  background: #17181c;
  color: #ffffff;
  font: 500 13px/1 system-ui, -apple-system, "Segoe UI", Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif;
  cursor: pointer;
}
.oba-import:hover { background: #3a3a3e; }
.oba-errors {
  margin: 0;
  flex: none;
  padding-left: 18px;
  color: #b42318;
  font-size: 12px;
  line-height: 16px;
}
.oba-credit {
  position: absolute;
  left: 16px;
  right: 16px;
  bottom: 12px;
  margin: 0;
  text-align: center;
  font-size: 12px;
  line-height: 16px;
  color: rgba(23, 24, 28, 0.62);
}
.oba-credit a { color: inherit; }
#oba-frame > .oba-credit {
  right: auto;
  width: 528px;
}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .oba-chip, .oba-swatch, .oba-import { transition: none; }
}
html[data-reduce-motion="1"] .oba-chip,
html[data-reduce-motion="1"] .oba-swatch,
html[data-reduce-motion="1"] .oba-import { transition: none; }

html, body { margin: 0; height: 100%; background: #f3f3f4; }
body { display: flex; justify-content: center; align-items: center; }
#oba-frame { position: relative; width: 960px; height: 720px; flex: none; }
#oba-root { width: 100%; height: 100%; }
</style>
</head>
<body>
<div id="oba-frame">
<div id="oba-root"></div>

wp=ja(by());var mt=ja(xu());var py=`.oba {
  width: 960px;
  height: 720px;
  display: flex;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  background: #f3f3f4;
  color: #17181c;
  font-family: system-ui, -apple-system, "Segoe UI", Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif;
}
.oba-stage {
  position: relative;
  flex: 1 1 auto;
  min-width: 0;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
}
.oba-avatar-host {
  width: 420px;
  max-width: calc(100% - 48px);
  aspect-ratio: 1;
}
.oba-compact {
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  min-width: 0;
}
.oba-compact .oba-stage { width: 100%; height: 100%; }
.oba-compact .oba-avatar-host {
  width: 100%;
  max-width: none;
  height: 100%;
}
.oba-panel {
  width: 400px;
  flex: none;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  height: 100%;
  padding: 16px;
  overflow: auto;
  border-left: 1px solid rgba(23, 24, 28, 0.08);
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 12px;
}
.oba-name {
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 13px;
  line-height: 18px;
  color: rgba(23, 24, 28, 0.72);
}
.oba-field {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 6px;
  margin: 0;
}
.oba-label {
  font-size: 11px;
  line-height: 14px;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.06em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: rgba(23, 24, 28, 0.52);
}
.oba-states {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
  gap: 6px;
}
.oba-row {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 6px;
}
.oba-chip {
  appearance: none;
  flex: none;
  height: 30px;
  padding: 0 10px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  border: 1px solid rgba(23, 24, 28, 0.12);
  background: #ffffff;
  color: #17181c;
  font: 500 13px/1 system-ui, -apple-system, "Segoe UI", Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif;
  cursor: pointer;
}
.oba-states .oba-chip { width: 100%; }
.oba-chip[aria-checked="true"] {
  background: #17181c;
  border-color: #17181c;
  color: #ffffff;
}
.oba-chip:hover { background: #f1f1f1; }
.oba-chip[aria-checked="true"]:hover { background: #3a3a3e; }
.oba-presets {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 8px;
  max-height: 216px;
  overflow: auto;
}
.oba-preset-group {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 6px;
}
.oba-group-name {
  font-size: 12px;
  line-height: 16px;
  color: rgba(23, 24, 28, 0.62);
}
.oba-swatches {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 8px;
}
.oba-swatch {
  width: 22px;
  height: 22px;
  padding: 0;
  border-radius: 999px;
  border: 1px solid rgba(23, 24, 28, 0.18);
  cursor: pointer;
}
.oba-swatch[aria-checked="true"] {
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px #f3f3f4, 0 0 0 4px #17181c;
}
.oba-json {
  width: 100%;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  flex: none;
  min-height: 64px;
  height: 64px;
  resize: vertical;
  border-radius: 10px;
  border: 1px solid rgba(23, 24, 28, 0.12);
  background: #ffffff;
  color: #17181c;
  padding: 8px 10px;
  font: 400 12px/16px ui-monospace, "SFMono-Regular", Menlo, Consolas, monospace;
}
.oba-import {
  appearance: none;
  align-self: flex-start;
  flex: none;
  height: 30px;
  padding: 0 12px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  border: 1px solid #17181c;
  background: #17181c;
  color: #ffffff;
  font: 500 13px/1 system-ui, -apple-system, "Segoe UI", Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif;
  cursor: pointer;
}
.oba-import:hover { background: #3a3a3e; }
.oba-errors {
  margin: 0;
  flex: none;
  padding-left: 18px;
  color: #b42318;
  font-size: 12px;
  line-height: 16px;
}
.oba-credit {
  position: absolute;
  left: 16px;
  right: 16px;
  bottom: 12px;
  margin: 0;
  text-align: center;
  font-size: 12px;
  line-height: 16px;
  color: rgba(23, 24, 28, 0.62);
}
.oba-credit a { color: inherit; }
#oba-frame > .oba-credit {
  right: auto;
  width: 528px;
}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .oba-chip, .oba-swatch, .oba-import { transition: none; }
}
html[data-reduce-motion="1"] .oba-chip,
html[data-reduce-motion="1"] .oba-swatch,
html[data-reduce-motion="1"] .oba-import { transition: none; }
```
